            while index < 120:
                values[index] = __wrela_pixels_f32_from_bits(R0_FRAME_SNAPSHOT.bits[28 + index])
                index = index + 1
            return values
        return [0.0; 120]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_frame_snapshot_post(renderer: usize) -> [f32; 4]:
        if renderer == 0:
            return [
                __wrela_pixels_f32_from_bits(R0_FRAME_SNAPSHOT.bits[148]),
                __wrela_pixels_f32_from_bits(R0_FRAME_SNAPSHOT.bits[149]),
                __wrela_pixels_f32_from_bits(R0_FRAME_SNAPSHOT.bits[150]),
                __wrela_pixels_f32_from_bits(R0_FRAME_SNAPSHOT.bits[151]),
            ]
        return [0.0; 4]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_frame_snapshot_meta(renderer: usize) -> [u64; 3]:
        if renderer == 0 and (R0_FRAME_SNAPSHOT.meta[1] & 65535) == 1:
            return [1, R0_FRAME_SNAPSHOT.meta[0], R0_FRAME_SNAPSHOT.meta[1] >> 16]
        return [0; 3]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_worker_assignment(renderer: usize, worker: u32) -> [u64; 7]:
        if renderer == 0 and worker == 0:
            return [1, 1084424192, 1086064224, 110393832, 0, 1, 0]
        return [0; 7]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_store_byte(renderer: usize, byte: usize, value: u8) -> bool:
        shift = (byte % 8).to[u64]() * 8
        mask = 255.to[u64]() << shift
        if renderer == 0 and byte.to[u64]() >= 0 and byte.to[u64]() < 16384:
            word = (byte.to[u64]() - 0).to[usize]() / 8
            old = R0_DEBUG_FRAMEBUFFER_CHUNK_0.words[word]
            R0_DEBUG_FRAMEBUFFER_CHUNK_0.words[word] = (old & (mask ^ 18446744073709551615)) | (value.to[u64]() << shift)
            return true
        return false

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_load_byte(renderer: usize, byte: usize) -> [u64; 2]:
        shift = (byte % 8).to[u64]() * 8
        if renderer == 0 and byte.to[u64]() >= 0 and byte.to[u64]() < 16384:
            word = (byte.to[u64]() - 0).to[usize]() / 8
            return [1, (R0_DEBUG_FRAMEBUFFER_CHUNK_0.words[word] >> shift) & 255]
        return [0; 2]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_reset(renderer: usize) -> bool:
        if renderer == 0:
            pass
            word_0: usize = 0
            @budget(bound=2048)
            while word_0 < 2048:
                R0_DEBUG_FRAMEBUFFER_CHUNK_0.words[word_0] = 0
                word_0 = word_0 + 1
            return true
        return false

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_write(renderer: usize, pixel: u32, r: u8, g: u8, b: u8, a: u8) -> bool:
        if renderer == 0:
            if pixel.to[u64]() >= 2048:
                return false
            marker = 8192 + pixel.to[usize]()
            marker_value = __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_load_byte(0, marker)
            if marker_value[0] != 1 or marker_value[1] != 0:
                return false
            byte = pixel.to[usize]() * 4
            if not __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_store_byte(0, byte, r) or not __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_store_byte(0, byte + 1, g) or not __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_store_byte(0, byte + 2, b) or not __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_store_byte(0, byte + 3, a) or not __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_store_byte(0, marker, 1):
                return false
            return true
        return false

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_byte(renderer: usize, byte: usize) -> [u64; 2]:
        if renderer == 0 and byte < 8192:
            return __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_load_byte(0, byte)
        return [0; 2]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_pixel_written(renderer: usize, pixel: u32) -> bool:
        if renderer == 0 and pixel.to[u64]() < 2048:
            value = __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_load_byte(0, 8192 + pixel.to[usize]())
            return value[0] == 1 and value[1] == 1
        return false

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_word_r0(word: usize) -> [u64; 2]:
        if word.to[u64]() >= 0 and word.to[u64]() < 2048:
            return [1, R0_DEBUG_FRAMEBUFFER_CHUNK_0.words[word - 0] ]
        return [0; 2]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_digest_r0() -> [u64; 5]:
        marker_word: usize = 0
        @budget(bound=256)
        while marker_word < 256:
            marker = __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_word_r0(1024 + marker_word)
            if marker[0] != 1 or marker[1] != 72340172838076673:
                return [0; 5]
            marker_word = marker_word + 1
        h0: u64 = 1469598103934665603
        h1: u64 = 1099511628211
        h2: u64 = 7809847782465536322
        h3: u64 = 1609587929392839161
        byte: usize = 0
        @budget(bound=8192)
        while byte < 8192:
            packed = __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_word_r0(byte / 8)
            if packed[0] != 1:
                return [0; 5]
            octet: usize = 0
            @budget(bound=8)
            while octet < 8 and byte < 8192:
                value = (packed[1] >> (octet * 8).to[u64]()) & 255
                h0 = (h0 ^ value) *% 1099511628211
                h1 = (h1 ^ (value +% byte.to[u64]())) *% 14029467366897019727
                h2 = (h2 +% value) *% 11400714785074694791
                h3 = (h3 ^ (value << (byte % 8).to[u64]())) *% 9650029242287828579
                byte = byte + 1
                octet = octet + 1
        return [1, h0, h1, h2, h3]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_digest(renderer: usize) -> [u64; 5]:
        if renderer == 0:
            return __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_digest_r0()
        return [0; 5]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_alpha_samples_r0() -> u64:
        word_0 = __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_word_r0(527)
        word_1 = __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_word_r0(528)
        word_2 = __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_word_r0(532)
        if word_0[0] != 1 or word_1[0] != 1 or word_2[0] != 1:
            return 18446744073709551615
        alpha_0 = (word_0[1] >> 56) & 255
        alpha_1 = (word_1[1] >> 24) & 255
        alpha_2 = (word_2[1] >> 24) & 255
        return alpha_0 | (alpha_1 << 8) | (alpha_2 << 16)

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_alpha_samples(renderer: usize) -> u64:
        if renderer == 0:
            return __wrela_pixels_p7_framebuffer_alpha_samples_r0()
        return 18446744073709551615

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_reset(renderer: usize, worker: u32, generation: u64) -> bool:
        if renderer == 0 and worker == 0:
            R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_HEADER.words[0] = generation
            slot: usize = 1
            @budget(bound=7)
            while slot < 8:
                R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_HEADER.words[slot] = 0
                slot = slot + 1
            return true
        return false

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_charge(renderer: usize, worker: u32, slot: usize, amount: u64) -> bool:
        if slot >= 7:
            return false
        if renderer == 0 and worker == 0:
            if (slot == 0 and amount > 26) or (slot == 1 and amount > 722) or (slot == 4 and amount > 482840) or (slot == 5 and amount > 26) or (slot == 6 and amount > 482839):
                return false
            index = slot + 1
            before = R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_HEADER.words[index]
            after = before +% amount
            if after < before:
                return false
            R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_HEADER.words[index] = after
            return true
        return false

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_counter(renderer: usize, worker: u32, slot: usize) -> [u64; 2]:
        if slot >= 7:
            return [0; 2]
        if renderer == 0 and worker == 0:
            return [1, R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_HEADER.words[slot + 1]]
        return [0; 2]

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_load_certified_run_word(renderer: usize, worker: u32, record: u32, word: usize) -> [u64; 2]:

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_store_coverage(renderer: usize, worker: u32, corridor: bool, record: u32, read values: [i64; 8]) -> bool:
        if values[0] < 0 or values[0] >= values[1] or values[1] > 65535 or values[2] < 0 or values[2] > 4294967295 or values[3] < 0 or values[3] > 4294967295 or values[4] < -2147483648 or values[4] > 2147483647 or values[5] < -2147483648 or values[5] > 2147483647 or values[6] < 0 or values[6] > 4294967295 or values[7] < 0 or values[7] > 4294967295:
            return false
        word0 = values[0].to[u64]() | (values[1].to[u64]() << 16)
        word1 = values[2].to[u64]() | (values[3].to[u64]() << 32)
        word2 = (values[4] & 4294967295).to[u64]() | ((values[5] & 4294967295).to[u64]() << 32)
        word3 = values[6].to[u64]() | (values[7].to[u64]() << 32)
        return (
            __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_store_record_word(renderer, worker, corridor, record, 0, word0)
            and __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_store_record_word(renderer, worker, corridor, record, 1, word1)
            and __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_store_record_word(renderer, worker, corridor, record, 2, word2)
            and __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_store_record_word(renderer, worker, corridor, record, 3, word3)
        )

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_store_certified_run(renderer: usize, worker: u32, record: u32, read values: [i64; 8], read model: [i64; 8], read normal: [i64; 6], read slacks: [i64; 2], sample_meta: i64) -> bool:
        return values[0] >= 0 and values[0] < values[1] and values[1] <= 65535 and record < 4294967295

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_store_root(renderer: usize, worker: u32, record: u32, read values: [i64; 8]) -> bool:
        if values[0] < -2147483648 or values[0] > 2147483647 or values[1] < -2147483648 or values[1] > 2147483647 or values[0] > values[1] or values[2] < 0 or values[2] > 4294967295 or values[3] < 0 or values[3] > 4294967295 or values[4] < 0 or values[4] > 4294967295 or values[5] < 0 or values[5] > 1 or values[6] < 0 or values[6] > 255 or values[7] < 0 or values[7] > 2147483647:
            return false
        word0 = (values[0] & 4294967295).to[u64]() | ((values[1] & 4294967295).to[u64]() << 32)
        word1 = values[2].to[u64]() | (values[3].to[u64]() << 32)
        word2 = values[4].to[u64]() | (values[5].to[u64]() << 32) | (values[6].to[u64]() << 40)
        word3 = values[7].to[u64]()
        if renderer == 0 and worker == 0 and record.to[u64]() >= 0 and record.to[u64]() < 2888:
            base = (record.to[usize]() - 0.to[usize]()) * 4
            R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_ROOTS_CHUNK_0.words[base] = word0
            R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_ROOTS_CHUNK_0.words[base + 1] = word1
            R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_ROOTS_CHUNK_0.words[base + 2] = word2
            R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_ROOTS_CHUNK_0.words[base + 3] = word3
            return true
        return false

    pub fn __wrela_pixels_p7_workspace_load_root(renderer: usize, worker: u32, record: u32) -> [i64; 9]:
        if renderer == 0 and worker == 0 and record.to[u64]() >= 0 and record.to[u64]() < 2888:
            base = (record.to[usize]() - 0.to[usize]()) * 4
            word0 = R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_ROOTS_CHUNK_0.words[base]
            word1 = R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_ROOTS_CHUNK_0.words[base + 1]
            word2 = R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_ROOTS_CHUNK_0.words[base + 2]
            word3 = R0_WORKER_0_WORKSPACE_ROOTS_CHUNK_0.words[base + 3]
            lo = (word0 & 4294967295).to[i64]()
            hi = (word0 >> 32).to[i64]()
            if lo >= 2147483648:
                lo = lo - 4294967296
            if hi >= 2147483648:
                hi = hi - 4294967296
            return [1, lo, hi, (word1 & 4294967295).to[i64](), (word1 >> 32).to[i64](), (word2 & 4294967295).to[i64](), ((word2 >> 32) & 1).to[i64](), ((word2 >> 40) & 255).to[i64](), word3.to[i64]()]
        return [0; 9]
